([murphyjoseph/_playground](https://github.com/murphyjoseph/_playground/tree/HEAD/skills/beta-agent-architect))
- **beta-art-ozellik**: Beta Art Privat arşiv sitesine (apps/beta-art-archive) yeni bir özellik/sistem eklerken uygulanan kurulum disiplini — dürüst v1 (statik sitede backend yoksa cihaz-içi), uydurma yasağı, 8 dil, tema tokenları, komisyon-ve-mva.md para modeli, ziyaretçi/yönetici sistem ayrımı, Playwright doğrulama ve günlük+commit+deploy teslim zinciri. Kullanıcı bu depoda "sistem kur", "özellik ekle", satış/davranış/öneri/panel/grafik/ödeme/hesap gibi bir şey istediğinde MUTLAKA bu beceriyi yükle — kullanıcı beceriyi adıyla anmasa bile. QBLOGG değil, Beta Art (beta-art-archive) işidir. ([andersenbetul-alt/BETA-ART-PRIVAT](https://github.com/andersenbetul-alt/BETA-ART-PRIVAT/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/beta-art-ozellik))
- **beta-assess-quantexa**: BETA - assess a Quantexa TM implementation against BRDs/TSDs - requirements-to-artifact traceability with a Quantexa knowledge base ([danieledge/virtual-surv-IT](https://github.com/danieledge/virtual-surv-IT/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/beta-assess-quantexa))
- **beta-binomial-cna-state-mixture-inference**: Use when phased allelic counts (integer 'AD'/'DP' layers) are available for scRNA-seq cells and you need to infer per-cell, per-bin allele-imbalance CNA state probabilities that are robust to sparse, low-coverage variant counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-cna-state-mixture-inference))
- **beta-binomial-differential-methylation-testing**: Use when you have a clustered-region methylation counts table (combined.csv with chr, start, end, CpG, per-sample -N and -X columns) and need to identify DMRs between two or more sample groups from MethylC-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-differential-methylation-testing))
- **beta-binomial-emission-probability-computation**: Use when an HMM-style methylation deconvolution (e.g. DXM's Viterbi decoding over user-defined regions) needs emission probabilities for observed fractional methylation plus read coverage at each CpG of a region. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-emission-probability-computation))
- **beta-binomial-enriched-window-testing**: Use when analyzing eCLIP/CLIP-seq FASTQs that have matched input controls and at least 2 CLIP replicates, and you need reproducible, annotated binding-site windows rather than peak calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-enriched-window-testing))
- **beta-binomial-enrichment-testing-with-gc-overdispersion-correction**: Use when detecting significant RBP binding to annotated repetitive elements or transcriptomic windows from eCLIP data with matched inputs, especially multi-mapping/repeat targets that peak callers miss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-enrichment-testing-with-gc-overdispersion-correction))
- **beta-binomial-hierarchical-dmr-testing**: Use when Bismark-aligned MethylC-seq reads must be tested for region-level differential methylation between two sample groups (e.g., IMR90/H1 replicates) with strict false-positive control, such that self-replicate contrasts yield minimal DMRs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-hierarchical-dmr-testing))
- **beta-binomial-mixture-classification**: Use when estimating per-cell per-gene allelic expression states (P/B/M) from low-coverage allele-specific scRNA-Seq counts (e.g., F1 hybrid CAST×B6 data), where U-shaped allelic-proportion distributions and sampling zeros require borrowing information across cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-mixture-classification))
- **beta-binomial-mixture-modeling**: Use when comparing PSI / splice-supporting read counts between two groups of LRTS samples and plain binomial tests show overdispersion, so within-group variability must be modeled before calling differential splicing events. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/beta-binomial-mixture-modeling))
- **beta-binomial-psi-estimation**: Use when you have a cell-by-isoform count matrix from single-cell Nanopore long reads and need to estimate inter-cell splicing heterogeneity (phi) versus psi at exon/splice sites, distinguishing isoform co-expression within cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-psi-estimation))
- **beta-binomial-read-count-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic bulk DNA and single-cell RNA read counts with known ground-truth clonal trees (Z, Ps) to quantify reconstruction error of tumor phylogeny inference methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-read-count-simulation))
- **beta-binomial-regression-modeling**: Use when analyzing tiled transcriptomic (or repetitive-element) windows from eCLIP data with matched IP and input read-start counts, and you need GC-bias-aware enrichment p-values accounting for count overdispersion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-regression-modeling))
- **beta-binomial-zero-inflation-modeling**: Use when single-cell mutation read counts (e.g., Rs/Xs matrices) show excess zeros from transcriptional bursting or dropout, and you need overdispersed counts modeling that decomposes zeros rather than a plain binomial likelihood. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/beta-binomial-zero-inflation-modeling))
- **beta-break-bot**: When a new Xcode or OS beta drops, rebuilds every app in the portfolio against the new SDK and reports compile errors, new deprecations, and new warnings per app. Use when a beta is released or when asked "what does beta N break". Read-only toward the apps — it reports, it never fixes. ([rshankras/indie-app-autopilot](https://github.com/rshankras/indie-app-autopilot/tree/HEAD/agents/beta-break-bot) · ★3)
- **beta-carryover-disabled-audit**: Audit the dashboard's Beta check "Carry over regressions, disabled in previous version" (beta.carryover-disabled-previous) — open regressions affecting the current Beta whose previous version is marked disabled, meaning the regressing change was not enabled in Release — and report which regressors are still off for Release in the current Beta branch code, which are now enabled (and the bug that enabled them), and which could not be determined. Use when asked things like "are the disabled carry-overs still disabled", "will these regressions ship in the next release", "audit the carry over regr… ([mozilla/Relman](https://github.com/mozilla/Relman/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/beta-carryover-disabled-audit) · ★3)
- **beta-cell-marker-module-correlation-analysis**: Use when a single-cell dataset annotated by condition (e.g. healthy vs mSTZ) yields per-cell confidence scores and you want to test whether confidence tracks a graded molecular state, via Spearman correlation against gene-module scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/beta-cell-marker-module-correlation-analysis))
- **beta-cell-subset-construction-pancreas**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq integration methods (scETM, Harmony, Scanorama, Seurat, LIGER, scVI, scVAE-GM) for over-correction, by removing a cell type (beta cells) from all Human Pancreas batches except CelSeq2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/beta-cell-subset-construction-pancreas))
- **beta-data**: Read what beta testers did (scans, events, ratings, who came through which invite link, request log, shutter captures, photos) from any machine, through the read-only admin routes on the beta server. Use whenever a question needs real tester data rather than a guess. ([Aurikology/shin](https://github.com/Aurikology/shin/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/beta-data))
- **beta-diversity-analysis-of-16s-microbiome-data**: Use when a genus-level 16S rRNA abundance table (e.g., Greengenes-collapsed ASVs from QIIME 2) exists and the goal is to quantify and visualize compositional dissimilarity between sample groups via NMDS ordination of Bray–Curtis dissimilarities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/beta-diversity-analysis-of-16s-microbiome-data))
- **beta-diversity-computation**: Use when evaluating whether OTU tables (e.g., from VSEARCH, SWARM, CROP, DADA2 at multiple clustering levels) have realistic sample-to-sample composition, by comparing their beta-diversity to an independent field-survey reference value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/beta-diversity-computation))
- **beta-diversity-distance-analysis**: Use when comparing gut microbial community composition between treatment groups (e.g., control vs tylosin) from a 16S feature table with taxonomic annotations and a phylogenetic tree, and you need distance matrices plus covariate-adjusted MiRKAT beta-diversity p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/beta-diversity-distance-analysis))
- **beta-diversity-distance-matrix-computation**: Use when a rarefied feature/OTU table (result/otutab_rare.txt), sample metadata with a Group column, and an OTU tree (result/otus.tree) are available and the goal is beta-diversity distance matrices, a PCoA plot, and pairwise group p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/beta-diversity-distance-matrix-computation))
- **beta-diversity-geographic-correlation**: Use when sample sets span multiple geographic sites and you need to test whether community composition turnover (beta-diversity) tracks geographic distance, e.g. isolation-by-distance in aquaculture or similar spatial metabarcoding designs.
